# Worklog — UDP CRM — Multi-Brand CRM (UDP: Unimasi, Segia Tech, Erfo Multimedia, Unicam Studio)

## Konvensi desain (WAJIB diikuti semua agent)
- Stack: Next.js 16 App Router + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui (komponen ada di `src/components/ui`) + lucide-react + recharts + zustand + sonner (toast via `import { toast } from "sonner"`).
- DILARANG memakai warna indigo/blue sebagai warna utama. Aksen utama: zinc-900 untuk tombol primer, warna brand: Unimasi `#ea580c` (oranye), Segia Tech `#059669` (emerald), Erfo Multimedia `#e11d48` (rose), Unicam Studio `#7c3aed` (violet). Won = emerald, Lost = red-600, Nurture = slate, Hot = red-600, Warm = amber-500, Cold = cyan-600.
- Background halaman: `bg-zinc-100` (di luar area konten). Kartu: `rounded-xl border bg-white shadow-sm`, padding `p-4`/`p-6`, gap `gap-4`/`gap-6`.
- Semua list panjang: `max-h-96 overflow-y-auto` + custom scrollbar (`.crm-scroll` di globals.css).
- Ikon lucide-react saja. Font default Geist. Teks UI BAHASA INDONESIA.
- Setiap module adalah SATU file komponen client di `src/components/crm/` dengan signature: `export default function XModule()` (atau menerima props dari shell). Fetch data memakai `api` dari `@/lib/crm/api-client` dan store dari `@/lib/crm/store` (`useCrmStore` berisi user, brands, activeBrandFilter).
- Helper format: `formatCurrency, formatCurrencyFull, formatDate, formatDateTime, timeAgo, initials, parseJsonArray` dari `@/lib/crm/utils`. Konstanta pipeline/roles/channels: `@/lib/crm/constants` (PIPELINE_STAGES, stageLabel, stageColor, LOST_REASONS, NURTURE_SEGMENTS, CHANNELS, ROLES, BRAND_SERVICES, TEMPERATURES, PRIORITIES).
- Type data: `@/lib/crm/types` (OpportunityDTO, InteractionDTO, TaskDTO, dst).
- Responsive wajib: grid mobile-first (`grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4`), tabel → overflow-x-auto.
- JANGAN mengedit file milik agent lain: page.tsx, layout.tsx, globals.css, src/lib/**, src/app/api/** hanya milik main agent kecuali ditugaskan.

## Arsitektur
- Single page app: user hanya melihat route `/` (src/app/page.tsx) dengan navigasi state via zustand (`activeModule`). JANGAN membuat route halaman baru.
- API routes lengkap (sudah jalan & terverifikasi curl): bootstrap, auth/login, brands, users, dashboard, opportunities (+[id], +[id]/summary AI), contacts (+[id], +merge), companies, interactions, tasks (+[id]), projects, invoices (GET + add_payment), audit-logs, identify, inbox (+convert).
- DB: Prisma + SQLite `db/custom.db`, schema di `prisma/schema.prisma` — sudah di-push, sudah di-seed (4 brand, 7 user PIN 1234, 10 company, 12 contact, 21 opportunity semua stage, 5 raw lead inbox, task, note, 3 project + milestone, 5 invoice + payment, 5 template follow-up, audit log).
- Login demo: pilih persona → PIN `1234`. Role: super_admin, director, marketing, finance, production, client.

## API contract ringkas (response JSON)
- GET /api/dashboard → { kpi{totalLeads,openLeads,pipelineValue,weightedPipeline,winRate,wonValue,avgResponseHours,overdueTasks,unassignedLeads,outstandingInvoices}, funnel[{stage,count,value}], byBrand[{brandId,name,color,leads,won,value}], byChannel[{channel,count}], byCountry[{country,count}], forecast[{bucket,value}], lostReasons[{reason,count}], marketingPerf[{name,leads,won,avgResponseHours}], recentAudit[...], pipelineTrend[{label,created,won}], projectsAtRisk, productionCapacity }
- GET /api/opportunities?stage&brandId&q&owner → { opportunities: OpportunityDTO[] (include brand, contact{company}, company) }
- GET /api/opportunities/:id → { opportunity (include interactions[], tasks[], notes[], projects[milestones], invoices), related[] }
- PATCH /api/opportunities/:id body { ...fields, actorName, actorRole } → stage change ternormalisasi + audit log; stage=won auto-buat project+milestone+invoice DP; stage=lost wajib lostReason → 400 jika kosong
- POST /api/inbox/convert body { interactionId, action:"link"|"new", contactId?, contact?, opportunity{brandId,title,...}, actorName }
- GET /api/inbox → { leads: InteractionDTO & { slaHours, candidates[] } }
- POST /api/identify body { email?, whatsapp?, fullName?, companyName? } → { candidates[{contactId,score,reasons[],contact}] }
- POST /api/contacts/merge body { primaryId, duplicateId, actorName }

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: Fondasi — schema DB, seed, core libs, API layer

Work Log:
- Desain schema Prisma multi-brand: Brand, User, Company, Contact, Opportunity, Interaction, Task, Note, Invoice, Payment, Project, Milestone, AuditLog, FollowUpTemplate (company-centric & contact-centric, opportunity/transaksi berkonteks brand).
- db push + generate client (SQLite db/custom.db).
- Lib: constants (12 stage pipeline standar + lost reasons + nurture segments + brand services), utils (normalisasi phone/email/domain, nameSimilarity, format currency IDR), types DTO, server helpers (logAudit, handleWonTransition → project+milestone+invoice DP otomatis, findMatchCandidates identity matching).
- Seed realistis Bahasa Indonesia: 4 brand, 7 user, 10 company, 12 contact (termasuk 1 pasangan duplikat Ratna utk demo merge), 21 opportunity tersebar 12 stage (3 won, 3 lost dengan alasan, 2 nurture), 5 raw lead inbox multi-kanal, task, notes, 3 project + milestones, 5 invoice (paid/partial/overdue) + payments, 5 follow-up template, audit logs.
- API 20+ endpoint terverifikasi curl: bootstrap OK, dashboard KPI OK (pipeline 1.81 M, winRate 50%), inbox 5 raw lead + SLA jam OK, identify matching Ratna score 85 OK.
- Fix bug: relasi Interaction.brand ditambahkan ke schema.

Stage Summary:
- Fondasi data & API selesai. Modul frontend dikembangkan oleh subagent dengan Task ID 2-a..3-c sesuai konvensi di atas.

Task ID: 2-b
Agent: frontend-styling-expert
Task: Dashboard Command Center module

Work Log:
- Baca worklog + audit kontrak: src/lib/crm/{api-client,store,constants,types,utils}.ts, komponen shadcn (Skeleton, Badge, Table, Button), verifikasi curl GET /api/dashboard (KPI pipeline 1.81 M, winRate 50%, byBrand 4, forecast, pipelineTrend 6 minggu, projectsAtRisk & productionCapacity ada di payload API).
- Buat src/components/crm/dashboard-module.tsx (satu-satunya file yang disentuh): "use client", default export DashboardModule, fetch `api.dashboard()` dengan loading skeleton shadcn, error handling try/catch + toast.error (sonner), state error + tombol coba lagi.
- Auto-refetch tiap 60 detik via setInterval (cleanup di useEffect) + tombol refresh manual (RefreshCw, ikon spin saat loading); footer "Data diperbarui {timeAgo(fetchTime)}" + keterangan auto-refresh.
- Header: judul Command Center + subtitle "Ringkasan eksekutif lintas brand", badge "{n} brand aktif" (dari useCrmStore, fallback byBrand), kartu user (initials + role label dari ROLES).
- 6 KPI card (grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4): Pipeline Value, Weighted Forecast, Win Rate (Trophy), Won Value (Handshake), Avg Response (Timer + badge SLA hijau <=8 jam / amber / merah >24 jam), Outstanding Invoice (ReceiptText); label uppercase text-xs text-zinc-500, angka text-2xl font-bold, ikon rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 p-2; delta trend mini (% vs fetch sebelumnya via useRef, TrendingUp/Down, emerald/rose sesuai arah bagus); strip aksen kiri 4px warna brand pertama pada 4 kartu utama.
- Funnel Konversi: 12 stage dari PIPELINE_STAGES, bar horizontal proporsional (stageColor + stageLabel), count + value kanan.
- Chart recharts (ResponsiveContainer height tetap): BarChart "Forecast Weighted 30/60/90" fill #059669; AreaChart "Tren Pipeline 6 Minggu" created #ea580c + won #059669 (gradient defs, Legend, Tooltip styled, grid zinc).
- Pipeline per Brand: kartu grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 (dot warna brand, leads, badge won emerald, value open, bar progress won/leads, hover:shadow-md).
- Alasan Lost: bar horizontal #dc2626 + empty state emerald; Performa Marketing: tabel shadcn (Nama/Leads/Won/Win Rate/Avg Response) dengan highlight baris terbaik bg-amber-50 + badge amber "Top".
- Distribusi Kanal & Negara: dua list badge count, label kanal via CHANNELS mapping + fallback kapitalisasi, negara dengan ikon Globe; Produksi & Risiko: kartu Project at Risk (rose jika >0) + Kapasitas Produksi + list Audit Terbaru (actorName + badge action berwarna + entityLabel + timeAgo, max-h-64 overflow-y-auto crm-scroll).
- Keputusan teknis: tipe bersama DashboardData belum memuat projectsAtRisk/productionCapacity (padahal API mengirim) — dibuat tipe lokal DashboardFullData + normalizeDashboard() defensif, TANPA mengedit src/lib/crm/types.ts milik main agent.
- Validasi: bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 error di dashboard-module.tsx; bun run lint → bersih.

Stage Summary:
- Modul Command Center selesai di src/components/crm/dashboard-module.tsx (~520 baris, strict TS tanpa any, Bahasa Indonesia, tanpa warna indigo/blue — hanya zinc/emerald/amber/rose/orange/violet/cyan). Siap diimpor page shell: `import DashboardModule from "@/components/crm/dashboard-module"` → render saat activeModule "dashboard". Catatan untuk main agent: pertimbangkan menambah projectsAtRisk & productionCapacity ke interface DashboardData di src/lib/crm/types.ts agar tipe bersama selaras dengan payload API.

Work Log:

- Tulis ulang total src/components/crm/inbox-module.tsx (satu-satunya file yang disentuh, ~830 baris): "use client", default export InboxModule, strict TS tanpa any.
- Data & state: api.inbox({channel, brandId}) dengan filter kanal (Semua Kanal + CHANNELS) & brand (controlled dari store activeBrandFilter via setActiveBrandFilter — sinkron global), user/brands dari useCrmStore, refetch otomatis saat filter berubah + tombol refresh (RefreshCw spin saat loading), skeleton shadcn saat first load, error state + tombol Coba Lagi + toast.error.
- Header: judul Lead Inbox + subtitle "Semua lead baru lintas kanal — Instagram, WhatsApp, Email, Website", badge zinc-900 "{n} lead belum dikonversi", legend SLA 3 dot (emerald ≤4 jam / amber ≤24 jam / rose >24 jam).

KIRI: daftar kartu lead (bukan tabel) dalam max-h-96 overflow-y-auto crm-scroll — ikon kanal lingkaran warna (whatsapp emerald, email amber, instagram rose, website violet, phone cyan, meeting zinc, portal orange), nama sender, formatDateTime + timeAgo, badge kanal + brand (dot warna brand), konten line-clamp-2, badge SLA (slaBadge()), badge amber "Duplikat? {n} kandidat" + AlertTriangle, kartu terlambat border-l-4 border-l-rose-500 (selected ring rose agar aksen late tetap terlihat). - Empty state: kartu dashed Inbox "Tidak ada lead baru — semua sudah ditangani"; placeholder panel: "Belum ada lead dipilih".
- Kualitas: UI Bahasa Indonesia penuh, tanpa indigo/blue (zinc/emerald/amber/rose/violet/cyan/orange), kartu rounded-xl border bg-white shadow-sm, transition-colors hover, aria-label untuk tombol ikon (refresh, tutup) + progressbar a11y, sub-komponen internal (ChannelAvatar, BrandChip, StatCard, LeadCard, CandidateCard, ListSkeleton), channel meta fallback Inbox untuk kanal tak dikenal.
- Validasi: bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 error (exclude examples/skills); bun run lint → bersih; curl GET /api/inbox → 200 OK payload sesuai kontrak (tidak mengubah seed data).

Stage Summary:
- Modul Lead Inbox selesai di src/components/crm/inbox-module.tsx: daftar lead multi-kanal + SLA badge + warning duplikat, panel detail sticky dengan identifikasi identitas (skor + reasons) dan konversi lead → contact (baru / gabung existing) + opportunity via POST /api/inbox/convert. Filter kanal & brand tersambung ke store. Siap dirender shell saat activeModule "inbox" via import default InboxModule(). Work Log:
- Baca worklog + audit kontrak API via source & curl sebelum menulis: /api/tasks (GET params status/assignee/overdue, POST create), /api/projects (PATCH {id,progress,status,milestoneId,milestoneStatus} — milestone branch recompute progress server-side), /api/invoices (GET {invoices,aging{current,d30,d60,d90}} include payments+project, POST add_payment auto-status partial/paid + audit), /api/audit-logs ({logs}, filter entity/action/limit max 300), /api/brands, /api/users, /api/companies, /api/auth/login (terverifikasi: respons login TIDAK menyertakan companyName untuk user client).
- Ganti total 7 file placeholder di src/components/crm/ (satu-satunya file yang disentuh), semua "use client" + default export, TS strict tanpa any, skeleton shadcn + tombol "Muat ulang" (RefreshCw spin saat loading) + silent refetch setelah aksi + toast sonner di semua aksi:
  1) followups-module.tsx "Follow-up Center": filter Select status (Semua/Open/Done) + assignee (opsi unik dari data) + Switch "Hanya overdue" (semua via param server); stat strip Total open / Overdue (rose) / Due hari ini (amber) / Selesai minggu ini (emerald); task card: Checkbox api.updateTask done/open, badge tipe (follow_up=BellRing amber, meeting=Video violet, internal=Building2 zinc, admin=ClipboardList cyan), badge prioritas (urgent rose/high amber), due date + badge "Overdue" rose, opportunity (brand dot + judul, klik → toast.info("Buka dari modul Sales Pipeline")), avatar initials assignee; list dikelompokkan section heading Overdue/Hari ini/Mendatang/Selesai; Dialog tambah task (title, type, priority, assigneeName, dueDate date-input, opportunityId opsional) → api.createTask + actorName dari store.
  2) finance-module.tsx "Finance": 4 kartu aging (Belum jatuh tempo emerald / 1–30 amber / 31–60 orange / >60 rose, formatCurrency); filter status + brand (opsi dari store brands); tabel invoice overflow-x-auto: number font-mono semibold, perusahaan, brand dot, deskripsi truncate, total formatCurrencyFull, status badge (draft zinc/sent violet/partial amber/paid emerald/overdue rose), due merah jika lewat & belum paid, tombol "Catat Pembayaran" (Dialog: amount prefill = total − Σpayments, method Select transfer/cash/qris/credit_card berlabel Indonesia, reference) → api.addPayment actorName user.name; klik row → Sheet detail (info lengkap subtotal/pajak/notes/project, Progress bar paid/total %, riwayat payments jumlah+metode+referensi+tanggal); ringkasan bawah total invoiced/paid/outstanding dihitung dari data. Tipe lokal InvoiceWithProject (perluasan defensif — API mengirim relasi project, belum ada di tipe bersama).
  3) projects-module.tsx "Projects": filter status + brand; grid kartu (sm:2 xl:3): code font-mono, nama, brand dot+perusahaan, Progress shadcn + %, status badge (planning zinc "Perencanaan", in_progress amber "Berjalan", review violet, completed emerald), PM, contract value, deadline rose jika < 7 hari & belum completed, chips milestone (done strikethrough emerald / in_progress amber / pending zinc) — klik chip → konfirmasi via toast (sonner action button "Ya, selesaikan") → api.updateProject({id,milestoneId,milestoneStatus:"done"}) lalu silent refetch + sinkron sheet; klik kartu → Sheet detail + form update status (Select) & progress manual (0–100) → api.updateProject.
  4) portal-module.tsx "Client Portal": role client → resolve company via api.companies() (match name === user.companyName, fallback domain email ↔ website perusahaan karena /api/auth/login belum mengirim companyName; tanpa match → kartu "Hubungi admin untuk mengaitkan akun"); banner info hijau "Anda melihat data perusahaan Anda sendiri…"; ringkasan project company (api.projects({companyId})) kartu milestone+progress; invoice company (api.invoices({companyId})) dengan badge status + tombol "Lihat" → Sheet read-only (info + riwayat payment, TANPA aksi pembayaran); super_admin/director → Select company + badge "Mode Pratinjau Klien", tampilan sama.
  5) brands-module.tsx "Brand Configuration": grid kartu brand (api.brands()): logoEmoji besar, nama+slug font-mono, deskripsi, warna (dot + kode), badge currency, invoicePrefix, SLA "{n} jam", portalDomain, website link eksternal; kartu "Layanan {brand}" chips dari BRAND_SERVICES; filter layanan (semua layanan unik lintas brand); kartu dashed "+ Tambah Brand" → Dialog form (name, slug auto-slugify dari name, Input type=color + preview hex, logoEmoji, description, primaryCurrency Select, invoicePrefix, slaHours number) — TIDAK memanggil endpoint yang tidak ada: submit → toast.info("Konfigurasi brand baru tersedia di fase berikutnya — struktur DB sudah siap") + kartu pratinjau lokal dashed emerald; section bawah menggantikan template follow-up (endpoint belum ada) dengan "Konfigurasi Pipeline Standar (12 Stage)": 12 stage PIPELINE_STAGES + meaning + aktivitas wajib.
  6) users-module.tsx "User & Access": tabel users (api.users()) avatar avatarColor + nama + email + role badge (super_admin zinc-900, director amber, marketing rose, finance cyan, production lime, client zinc) + status Aktif/Nonaktif; filter role dengan count; Matriks Hak Akses: tabel MODULE_META (label+description) × ROLES dengan ikon Check/X (emerald/zinc) — sticky kolom modul, hover baris; kartu penjelasan per role (label + description dari ROLES + jumlah modul + jumlah user).
  7) audit-module.tsx "Audit Logs": filter entity (9 opsi) / action (8 opsi) / limit (50/100/200) + summary "{n} log · {n} aktor unik"; tabel dalam max-h-[70vh] overflow-y-auto crm-scroll (header sticky): Waktu formatDateTime + timeAgo kecil, Aktor avatar initials + nama + role badge kecil, Aksi badge (create emerald, update amber, delete rose, stage_change violet, merge cyan, login zinc, convert orange), Entity badge + entityLabel, Perubahan "{field}: old → new" mono kecil (old rose strikethrough, new emerald) fallback metadata truncate, IP + userAgent text-xs zinc-400 truncate; row hover bg-zinc-50.
- Keputusan teknis: (a) invoice/project relation & companyName belum ada di tipe bersama → perluasan tipe lokal defensif, TIDAK mengedit src/lib/**; (b) milestone done → server recompute progress → selalu silent refetch agar progress/status konsisten; (c) konfirmasi milestone memakai sonner toast action button (tanpa dialog ekstra).
- Validasi: bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 error (grep -v examples/skills); bun run lint → bersih; curl sanity /api/tasks, /api/invoices, /api/projects, /api/brands, /api/audit-logs, /api/companies → semua 200 JSON valid. Dev server tidak dimatikan/di-restart, tidak ada route baru.

Stage Summary:
- 7 modul selesai (~2.9k baris) di src/components/crm/{followups,finance,projects,portal,brands,users,audit}-module.tsx — UI Bahasa Indonesia, rounded-xl border bg-white shadow-sm, list panjang max-h + crm-scroll, ikon lucide-react, aria-label tombol ikon, tanpa indigo/blue, responsive mobile-first. Siap dipakai shell: import default masing-masing sudah ter-hook di app-shell (activeModule followups/finance/projects/portal/brands/users/audit). Catatan main agent: (1) /api/auth/login sebaiknya sertakan companyName untuk role client agar portal tidak perlu fallback domain; (2) POST /api/brands & /api/followup-templates belum ada — brands-module menampilkan pratinjau lokal + toast info fase berikutnya; (3) PATCH /api/projects mengembalikan snapshot sebelum recompute milestone → frontend melakukan silent refetch.

Task ID: 3-b
Agent: frontend-styling-expert
Task: Modul Contacts & Companies

Work Log:

- API: GET/PUT /api/opportunities/[id]/estimation (auto-create draft, kalkulasi server: totalCost→contingency→mgmtFee→discount→tax→margin/marginPct; submit=true membuat ApprovalRequest; sync revenue ke opportunity; tolak edit jika status pending_approval/approved), GET/POST /api/quotations (auto number brand.quotePrefix-YYYY-NNNN, items validator, totals server), PATCH /api/quotations/[id] (action: send→status sent+interaction timeline+opp stage estimation→proposal_sent; accept→verbal_agreement; convert_invoice→invoice draft baru + audit; update draft), GET/PATCH /api/approvals (RBAC: hanya director/super_admin boleh memutuskan → 403; approve mengubah estimation.status→approved+approvedBy).
- /api/brands kini support POST (slug auto, prefix, audit log). /api/followup-templates GET baru (dari seed). Dashboard API + pendingApprovals[]. Detail opportunity include estimation & quotations.
- api-client + types: EstimationDTO, QuotationDTO, ApprovalRequestDTO, FollowUpTemplateDTO, method getEstimation/saveEstimation/quotations/createQuotation/quotationAction/approvals/decideApproval/createBrand/followUpTemplates.
- Seed: estimasi draft virtual tour (margin 55%), estimasi pending approval live streaming (diskon 8%, margin di bawah target → approval request oleh Dewi), quotation UCS-2026-0001 (sent), quotation EFM-2026-0001 (accepted).
- Verifikasi curl: estimation PUT compute benar (margin 70.8% utk input tes), quotation convert_invoice → EFM-2026-INV-006, approvals marketing=403 director=200 approved, dashboard pendingApprovals=1. Reseed bersih.

Stage Summary:
- Backend Fase 2 lengkap & teruji. UI dikembangkan subagent Task 7-a (tab Estimasi+Quotation di opportunity-detail), 7-b (Antrean Approval dashboard), 7-c (wire POST brands + template follow-up).

  - Step 1 Input: drop-zone dashed label dgn <input type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv"> hidden + FileReader.readAsText (nama file tampil, input direset agar file sama bisa diunggah ulang); Textarea monospace min-h-36 dgn placeholder = template; tombol "Isi contoh" (outline sm) mengisi textarea; blok "Contoh format" <pre> mono berisi template 4 baris data (Hendra Wijaya utk demo dedupe skor 99, Budi Santoso ×2 WA sama utk demo intra-batch, "Tanpa Kontak" utk demo invalid); chip live "{n} baris terdeteksi" (rose + chip "Melebihi batas" bila >200) + tombol "Analisis Duplikat" (zinc-900, disabled saat 0 baris/over-limit, spinner "Menganalisis…"); footer Batal.

  - Step 3 Done: 4 stat chip besar (Baru emerald / Digabung violet / Dilewati zinc / Invalid rose) + baris "＋{n} perusahaan baru dibuat" bila companiesCreated > 0; footer "Impor lagi" (outline, RotateCcw, reset ke step 1) + "Selesai" (primary, tutup dialog). - [BARU] API `src/app/api/opportunities/import/route.ts` (POST): mode preview (commit=false) → validasi + resolusi tiap baris tanpa menulis DB, return `{ preview, summary: {total, valid, invalid, review} }`; mode commit → create opportunity valid + skip baris review/invalid, return `{ summary: {created, skipped, invalid}, results }`, SATU audit log per batch (action "import", entity "opportunity", entityId "import-batch", metadata count hasil). Row schema = 17 header ekspor lowercase; kolom `skor` & `dibuat` diabaikan (skor dihitung server via computeLeadScore saat GET — sama seperti POST/GET opportunity).
- Aturan validasi/resolusi: judul wajib → invalid; brand wajib resolusi nama case-insensitive → invalid "Brand tidak ditemukan: X"; kontak wajib resolusi fullName exact case-insensitive-trimmed (deletedAt null) → status "review" + error "Kontak tidak ditemukan: X" (review di-skip saat commit); perusahaan match Company case-insensitive → fallback companyId kontak; tahap label↔slug case-insensitive (label Inggris hasil stageLabel, slug mentah, + alias Indonesia: negosiasi/estimasi/proposal terkirim/terhubung/dll) → unknown default "new"; temperatur Hot/Warm/Cold → default cold; prioritas High/Medium/Low/Urgent → default medium; mata_uang default IDR; nilai_estimasi strip non-digit → number|null; probabilitas_pct clamp 0-100 default 50; target_close parse "dd MMM yyyy" bulan Indonesia (Jan Feb Mar Apr Mei Jun Jul Agu Sep Okt Nov Des), ISO yyyy-mm-dd, dd/mm/yyyy → Date|null. Batas 200 baris per impor. Row di-normalisasi kunci lowercase + lookup map in-memory (brand/contact/company) agar efisien.
- [BARU] `src/lib/crm/types.ts`: ImportOpportunityRowDTO, ImportOpportunityPreviewResponseDTO, ImportOpportunityCommitResultRowDTO, ImportOpportunityCommitResponseDTO (mirror DTO impor kontak).
- [BARU] `src/lib/crm/api-client.ts`: `api.importOpportunities({rows, commit, actorName, actorRole})` (mirror importContacts).
- [BARU] `src/components/crm/pipeline-module.tsx`: tombol "Impor CSV" (outline sm, ikon Upload, aria-label "Impor opportunity dari CSV") berdampingan dgn "Ekspor CSV" di toolbar; dialog `ImportOpportunitiesDialog` 2 langkah — (1) dropzone dashed (ikon Upload, helper text 17 kolom, maks 200 baris, file input .csv) → parse CSV client-side (salin parser murni contacts-module: strip BOM, quoted field escape "", CRLF/LF, deteksi separator ; atau ,; mapping via header lowercase atau posisi kolom) → langsung panggil preview API; (2) pratinjau: chip ringkasan (N Valid emerald-100/700, N Review amber-100/700, N Invalid red-100/700 + N baris), tabel scroll `max-h-96 overflow-y-auto crm-scroll` (judul/brand/kontak/tahap resolved/nilai formatCurrency/status chip + alasan error truncate dgn title attr); footer total counts + tombol primer "Impor {n} baris" (zinc-900, disabled bila valid=0/committing) + "Unggah ulang" + Batal. - Risiko/catatan: (1) dedupe antar-impor belum ada — mengunggah file sama dua kali membuat opportunity duplikat (title+brand+kontak); (2) resolusi kontak exact-name saja (tanpa fuzzy) — nama beda spasi/kapitalasi ekstrem masuk review, aman tapi bisa banyak review; (3) import masih premurah tanpa transaksi DB per baris (sama dgn pola impor kontak); (4) dev server sempat mati dua kali karena faktor eksternal (agent konkuren) — dihidupkan ulang; (5) parser CSV duplikat kecil di pipeline-module (sengaja: contacts-module tidak boleh diubah/diekspor).

Task ID: 15-b
Agent: frontend-styling-expert
Task: Notifikasi realtime via socket.io mini service (port 3005)

Work Log:
- Mini service BARU `mini-services/notif-service/` (project mandiri, package.json sendiri, `bun add socket.io@4.8.3` + `bun install` di folder itu): `index.ts` — socket.io Server di HTTP server hardcoded PORT 3005 (BUKAN process.env.PORT), `path: "/"` (konvensi gateway, selaras examples/websocket/server.ts), cors *. Event: `subscribe` {email, brandId?} → join room `${email}::${brandId ?? "all"}` + emit `subscribed` {room} (idempoten, socket = 1 room); `unsubscribe` → leave; `ping`→`pong`; disconnect → leave. Poll loop 15 dtk per room aktif → GET `http://localhost:3000/api/notifications?user=...` (+&brandId jika bukan "all") → hash sha1(`${unread}|${items.map(i=>i.key).join(",")}`) (field kunci = `key` di NotificationDTO) → berbeda dari baseline → `io.to(room).emit("notif:changed", {email, brandId, unread, at: ISO})`; observasi pertama per room = baseline tanpa emit; fetch gagal di-log sekali (flag reset saat sukses). Map room→Set<socketId>, room kosong dihapus + baseline dibuang. Graceful shutdown SIGTERM/SIGINT (close + clearInterval + io.close + failsafe 3 dtk). Dijalankan daemon double-fork python (pola Task 12, stdio → dev.log service agar log persisten) — selamat lintas tool call (PPID 1, terverifikasi berulang).
- Main project: `bun add socket.io-client@4.8.3` (satu-satunya dep yang disentuh).
- BARU `src/lib/crm/notif-socket.ts`: hook `useNotifSocket({email, brandId, enabled, onChanged})` → SATU koneksi lazy `io("/?XTransformPort=3005", { path: "/", transports: ["polling","websocket"] })`; on connect(+reconnect via manager) emit subscribe pakai ref (email/brandId/onChanged via ref — tanpa reconnect loop); on `notif:changed` → onChanged ref; re-subscribe saat email/brandId berubah (unsubscribe + subscribe); expose `{ connected }`; cleanup unmount = unsubscribe + removeAllListeners + close.
- `notification-center.tsx` (minimal-invasive): pakai hook (email + activeBrandFilter sama seperti api.notifications, enabled !!user); onChanged → `load(true)` yang SAMA dengan polling 60 dtk + dispatch CustomEvent; interval 60 dtk dipertahankan tapi skip tick saat realtime connected (realtimeRef — tanpa fetch dobel); footer diganti status realtime: dot `h-2 w-2 rounded-full` emerald `animate-pulse` + "Realtime aktif" saat connected, dot zinc + "Mode fallback · polling 60 dtk" saat putus (aria-live polite); export const NOTIF_CHANGED_EVENT = "crm:notif-changed".
- `dashboard-module.tsx` (satu listener kecil di NotificationBriefWidget, tanpa rewrite): addEventListener NOTIF_CHANGED_EVENT → load(true) — widget ringkas ikut segar saat push realtime.
- TEMUAN INFRA (penting): gateway XTransform ada di port 81 (`*:81`, di luar PID namespace) — dari page `fetch('/?XTransformPort=3005&EIO=4...')` via origin **localhost:3000** tetap dilayani Next (HTML, bukan handshake), jadi pola `io("/?XTransformPort=3005")` hanya nyambung bila app dibuka lewat origin gateway **http://localhost:81** (terbukti: handshake `0{"sid":...}` via :81 dan koneksi realtime sukses di browser). QA akhir memakai localhost:81.
- Dev server mati sendiri 2× di tengah sesi (gejala berulang yang sama dgn ronde 9/12) — di-restart 2× memakai pola double-fork terdokumentasi, tanpa db:push/reseed.
- QA (session crm15b, Sari/Direktur via localhost:81): baseline badge "9 belum dibaca" (title "9 notifikasi belum dibaca"); trigger: `POST /api/interactions` 21:53:35 (inbound whatsapp, brand Unimasi aktif, senderName+content) — catatan: sumber notif SLA hanya muncul bila waited > slaHours, jadi lead baru belum menaikkan badge; supaya deterministik ≤20 dtk, createdAt lead tsb dimundurkan 6 jam via script prisma one-off (6 > sla 4; masih di bawah ambang sweep sla 8 jam agar tak bikin task) → 21:54:25 log service `notif:changed → unread=10` → 21:54:35 badge 9→"9+" (title "10 notifikasi belum dibaca", chip "10 belum dibaca", item "SLA terlampaui: Rina Kusumawati (QA15)" ada di list & widget dashboard) TANPA reload — bukti timestamp lengkap; screenshot qa15-realtime.png. Resiliensi: kill service → footer langsung "Mode fallback · polling 60 dtk" (dot zinc) → restart daemon → auto-reconnect + re-subscribe `sari@grup.co.id::all` → footer "Realtime aktif" (dot emerald pulse). Console 0 error, page errors kosong. Setelah QA: data uji dihapus (interaction + 2 audit row via prisma one-off, unread kembali 9/10) — seed lain tidak disentuh.
- `bun run lint` 0 error; `bunx tsc --noEmit` bersih di src/ (noise hanya examples/ & skills/ pre-existing).

Stage Summary:
- Notifikasi kini push realtime end-to-end: mini service socket.io (port 3005, path "/") polling delta hash per room → push "notif:changed" → NotificationCenter refetch instan (fallback 60 dtk otomatis skip saat realtime aktif) → widget dashboard ikut via CustomEvent. Status koneksi terlihat user di footer popover (emerald pulse = realtime, zinc = fallback). Lint/tsc bersih, console bersih, QA badge 9→10 tanpa reload ≤20 dtk terverifikasi.
- Keputusan: (1) browser/QA harus lewat origin gateway localhost:81 (param XTransformPort tidak di-intercept di origin :3000 langsung); (2) trigger QA deterministik = POST lead + backdate createdAt −6 jam (sumber SLA butuh waited > slaHours — POST fresh saja tak mengubah badge; backdate 6 jam juga sengaja di bawah ambang sweep 8 jam agar tidak efek samping task); (3) hash pakai field `key` NotificationDTO; (4) room dikey `email::brandId` dan socket diganti room via unsubscribe+subscribe.
- Risiko/catatan: (1) `bun --hot` mengabaikan `process.exit()` — graceful shutdown menutup listener (port lepas, restart aman) tapi proses bisa menggantung → perlu `kill -9` utk bersih total; untuk produksi jalankan tanpa --hot (mis. `bun index.ts`); (2) mini service fetch HTTP biasa ke :3000 — jika dev server mati, ia hanya log sekali & terus mencoba (push terhenti, client fallback polling); (3) hanya SATU instance hook yang boleh memakai koneksi (kini hanya NotificationCenter); (4) dev server sandbox masih rentan mati sendiri (2× sesi ini) — cek `curl localhost:3000/api/brands` sebelum kerja.

Script tes socket dibiarkan di qa17-socket-stats.mjs (project root) utk dipakai ulang ronde berikutnya. Read-only feature — TIDAK ada cleanup data.

Stage Summary:
- Monitoring kesehatan end-to-end: (1) notif-service kini punya stats observability via socket "stats" (ack + event service:stats) tanpa menyentuh kontrak lama maupun HTTP server-nya; (2) GET /api/health probe server-side db+service+memori; (3) UI "Status sistem" di preferensi notifikasi menampilkan realtime service (uptime+ruangan via socket) & kesehatan server (db ms, probe service saat fallback), refresh per-buka panel tanpa interval.
- API contract GET /api/health → { status: "ok"|"degraded", db: { status: "up"|"down", ms: number|null }, notifService: { status: "up"|"down", detail: string }, uptimeSec, rssMb } — degraded bila db down/ms>500 ATAU service down; Cache-Control: no-store; tanpa auth.
- Socket stats contract: client emit "stats" (opsional ack cb) → ack payload ATAU event "service:stats" { startedAt(ISO), uptimeMs(number), activeRooms(number = rooms.size), totalEmits, pollCount, pollErrorCount, lastPollAt(ISO|null) }; produksi tetap io("/?XTransformPort=3005") path "/" — koneksi langsung :3005 hanya utk tes.
- Keputusan: (1) stats socket client pakai koneksi ephemeral forceNew (bukan share socket hook — notif-socket.ts milik main, tidak diizinkan disentuh); (2) baris probe "dari server" hanya muncul di mode fallback agar panel tetap compact saat realtime sehat; (3) db.ms dibulatkan 1 desimal, threshold lambat 500ms sesuai spec; (4) stats HANYA via socket — tanpa HTTP /health di mini-service (menghindari bentrok handshake).
- Risiko/catatan: (1) dev server sandbox rentan OOM (RAM 4GB, next-server ~1.8GB) — restart pattern perlu dijalankan siapa pun yang menemukan :3000 mati; pollErrorCount notif-service naik saat itu (bukan bug); (2) uptime/rooms di UI tidak live (diambil sekali per buka panel — sesuai spec); (3) probe notifService di /api/health pakai fetch HTTP mentah (400 = hidup) — bila gateway/service berubah path, update konstanta; (4) script qa17-socket-stats.mjs connect langsung :3005 — jangan dipakai di client produksi.

Work Log:
- Diagnosa feedback: (1) status "Terhubung" palsu — test lama hanya cek FORMAT; (2) balasan email tidak pernah benar-benar dikirim; (3) field IMAP tidak ada. Sandbox PROBE: smtp.gmail.com:465, imap.gmail.com:993, graph.facebook.com:443 semuanya REACHABLE → verifikasi nyata memungkinkan.
- Deps: + nodemailer (verify/sendMail), imapflow (login IMAP + fetch), @types/nodemailer. Prisma: Interaction.deliveryNote String?; ChannelConfig.lastEmailSyncAt DateTime?; db:push + RESTART dev (script /tmp/restart_dev.py).
- src/lib/crm/channel-verify.ts (BARU ~300 baris): verifyEmailChannel (nodemailer verify() = handshake+auth NYATA port 465 secure/587; lalu ImapFlow connect bila imapHost diisi — SMTP wajib lulus dulu, IMAP gagal → 422 dgn gabungan pesan), verifyWhatsAppChannel + verifyInstagramChannel (Graph API v21.0 GET /{id} — token & ID dicek beneran), withTimeout 8–12s, friendlyVerifyError (ENOTFOUND/ECONNREFUSED/timeout/535 auth/530/TLS cert/Graph 190→token invalid/100→ID salah dalam Bahasa operator), sendEmailViaSmtp (sendMail nyata), extractEmailAddress (parse email dr teks bebas).
- channels.ts: field email DIGANTI — SMTP (host/port/user/password) + IMAP (host/port/user/password opsional, kosongkan = sama dgn SMTP); preset Zoho/Gmail/Mailgun kini mengisi IMAP juga; panduan wizard email diubah ("Verifikasi nyata & aktifkan", checklist IMAP); DEMO_CONNECTIONS email + IMAP fields.
- API /api/channels POST: SEKARANG verifikasi NYATA SEBELUM create — gagal → 422 (config TIDAK dibuat, tidak ada lagi "terhubung palsu"); opsi skipVerification:true → isDemo=true + statusNote "TANPA verifikasi". Duplicate check 409 dipindah SEBELUM verify. PATCH [id] action test/update: pracheck format → verifyChannel NYATA; config isDemo → test melewati jaringan (jujur: "kredensial buatan, verifikasi dilewati", bukan error palsu). Semua hasil (lulus/gagal) masuk audit "uji nyata".
- Kirim nyata: src/lib/crm/email-delivery.ts (deliverEmailReply — kanal email connected non-demo → sendEmailViaSmtp; demo/belum terhubung → status "simulated" dgn note jujur). Dipakai POST /api/inbox/respond (jalur balas inbox) & POST /api/interactions. deliveryStatus kini: sent (nyata) / failed + note error / simulated (tanpa tick palsu). inbox-module handleSend: toast berbeda per status (sent=success "terkirim nyata", failed=error 8s dgn alasan, simulated=info penjelasan).
- IMAP sync: BARU POST /api/channels/email-sync — getMailboxLock("INBOX"), fetch since=lastEmailSyncAt (default 3 hari), envelope+bodyStructure; text/plain part dipilih (fallback text/html dibersihkan tag), dedupe externalId=Message-ID, buat interaction inbound (muncul sbg lead di inbox), update lastEmailSyncAt, audit "email_sync"; error IMAP → 502 dgn pesan ramah. Tombol ikon Mail "Tarik email masuk via IMAP" di header Lead Inbox + toast hasil (created/skipped).
- Wizard: final step kini ada checkbox amber "Sambungkan tanpa verifikasi nyata (mode demo)" (default OFF) + penjelasan default verifikasi nyata; error verifikasi (422) tampil INLINE dgn pesan asli server; layar sukses menampilkan "Hasil verifikasi: ..." (config.statusNote).
- QA Bukti nyata: (1) SMTP sink lokal scripts/dev-smtp-sink.ts (bun, port 3465, AUTH LOGIN, log DATA) — POST /api/channels ke sink → 201 "SMTP terverifikasi nyata (handshake + login OK)"; (2) balas lead procurement@metroland.co.id via /api/inbox/respond → deliveryStatus "sent" + sink menerima MAIL FROM/RCPT TO/DATA lengkap (RFC822 header+body di /tmp/smtp-sink.log); (3) Gmail password salah → 422 "SMTP: autentikasi gagal — Gmail wajib App Password" (RESPONS ASLI Gmail); (4) partial: SMTP sink OK + IMAP gmail salah → 422 "SMTP terverifikasi nyata ... tetapi IMAP: autentikasi gagal"; (5) email-sync tanpa IMAP host → 422 instruksi edit; (6) wizard browser: bad creds → alert inline pesan asli, centang demo → sukses "terhubung (demo)" dgn "Hasil verifikasi: TANPA verifikasi".
- TEMUAN PENTING data user: koneksi "Adib Test Email" (dibuat user saat eksperimen) kini dites nyata → status ERROR dgn diagnosis "SMTP: host tidak ditemukan — periksa ejaan host" (inilah kenapa email user tak pernah terkirim). Koneksi demo (WA/IG) dites → jujur dilewati, kembali connected. Fix tsc: imapflow 1.7.6 = getMailboxLock (bukan getLock), MessageStructureObject, internalDate Date|string, download.content = Readable stream (kumpulkan chunk).
- Mobile 390px overflowX=false; console 0 error; dev.log bersih; lint 0; tsc src 0. Screenshot: qa21-wizard-verify.png, qa21-channels.png, qa21-channels-mobile.png. DATA final: 4 koneksi — Email Bisnis (demo, Unimasi), IG (demo), Adib Test Email (ERROR jujur — contoh nyata), WA (demo). Sink QA tetap berjalan di 127.0.0.1:3465 (log /tmp/smtp-sink.log; matikan: pkill -f dev-smtp-sink).

Stage Summary:
- Klaim "Terhubung" sekarang BERARTI SESUATU: SMTP handshake+auth nyata, IMAP login nyata, Graph API cek token/ID nyata — tanpa verifikasi sukses, koneksi TIDAK dibuat (422). Jalur demo tetap ada tapi eksplisit (checkbox + badge + statusNote) — menjawab komplain "langsung dianggap terkoneksi padahal belum".

- IMAP kini bisa diisi (host/port/user/pass) + preset; kosong = sinkron mati (dijelaskan).

- src/components/crm/client-token-portal.tsx (BARU, "use client", standalone tanpa chrome CRM): fetch portalApi.byToken di useEffect; loading = skeleton halaman penuh; error = kartu elegan tengah layar (ShieldAlert dalam lingkaran rose-100, judul "Tautan tidak tersedia", pesan server, teks "Minta tautan baru kepada tim Unimasi Group…"). Sukses: bg-zinc-100, container max-w-3xl; Header card (ikon Building2 lingkaran orange-100 + nama perusahaan bold xl + industry/city muted; Badge "Portal Klien" bg-zinc-900 + label token; sub-teks "Akses aman tanpa login…"); 3 seksi: (a) Dokumen & MoU — grid sm:grid-cols-2, kartu title bold + chip kind (MoU=orange outline / Dokumen=zinc), anchor unduh href=fileData download=fileName + ukuran KB, atau ExternalLink url target _blank rel=noopener, content line-clamp-3, tanggal; (b) Catatan Rapat — timeline border-l dgn titik orange, tanggal meetingAt (formatDateTime), peserta chip outline maks 4 + "+N", title bold, content whitespace-pre-wrap; (c) Deliverable & Review — per project card (code mono · nama, Badge status proyek, Progress% indikator orange, target dueDate), baris deliverable ikon Link2/Paperclip + status badge (pending zinc "Menunggu Review" / approved emerald "Disetujui" / revision amber "Revisi Diminta") + note; status pending → form inline Input "Nama Anda" (wajib, validasi + border-rose bila kosong) & komentar opsional + tombol "Setujui" (bg-emerald-600 hover:emerald-700) & "Minta Revisi" (outline amber) ≥44px → portalApi.review → toast.success "Terima kasih, review tersimpan." + refetch; error toast.error; baris ter-review menampilkan status baru + komentar + "oleh {reviewedBy} · waktu". Footer tengah ikon Lock "Tautan aman khusus untuk {perusahaan}. Jangan bagikan ke pihak lain." Palet zinc/orange/emerald/amber (tanpa indigo/blue/violet). Accessibility: label sr-only utk input, aria-hidden ikon, semantic main/section/footer.

AppShell : LoginScreen; hook user/setBrands & useEffect bootstrap tetap dipanggil tanpa syarat (early-return DI DALAM effect bila portal — hook tidak lompat cabang); Page membungkus <Suspense fallback={div bg-zinc-100}> (wajib utk useSearchParams) + Toaster seperti semula.

Work Log:
- countries.ts BARU (227 negara: UN members + wilayah umum): {name, iso2, dial(+), currency ISO 4217}, urut alfabetis via foldName (diakritik-ditolong) — diverifikasi script (sorted: true); findCountry(nameOrDial) case-insensitive + toleran diakritik + dial dengan/tanpa "+".
- utils.ts: normalizePhone(raw, dial?) — tanpa dial perilaku lama utuh (08→628, kompatibel semua caller existing); dgn dial: strip non-digit, buang 0 depan, nomor yang sudah berawalan digit dial dipertahankan, hasil = dial + nasional. 11 kasus uji OK.
- contacts-module: CountryCombobox (Popover+Command, "Cari negara…", dial + badge currency, Check indikator) menggantikan input teks Negara di ContactFormFields & CreateCompanyDialog; di dialog perusahaan memilih negara → defaultCurrency otomatis = country.currency (opsi currency yang belum ada di CURRENCY_OPTIONS ditambahkan dinamis; override manual mematikan status auto-sync; helper text "Mata uang otomatis mengikuti negara").

- Seed fresh 100% sesuai struktur data terkini (brief, task multi-assignee JSON, katalog, estimasi, CR, invoice) dan aman: penghapusan berurutan cascade-safe + artefak QA dibersihkan setelah pengujian.
- Catatan: latar push VAPID tetap di .env (tetap hidup); risiko: sesi browser lama yang masih menyimpan user lama (mis. sari@grup.co.id) akan 404 pada notifikasi polling hingga user login ulang (persist key sudah diganti sehingga cache lama teraba).

Work Log:
- INPUT USER: (1) lengkapi dulu fitur modul keuangan yang belum lengkap — pastikan sinkron brand & kop surat; (2) kemudian ubah "Matriks Hak Akses per Modul" agar bisa dikonfigurasi admin (bukan hardcode) dengan level otorisasi per fungsi modul (read-only, write, dll).
- AUDIT FINANCE (sebelum): hanya ada daftar invoice + aging + catat/kirim/batalkan + quotation (list, aksi, cetak dgn kop). TIDAK ADA: buat invoice manual (di luar project/quotation), edit draft, cetak invoice (kop surat hanya utk quotation), ekspor CSV, koreksi pembayaran; status "overdue" tak pernah ter-set otomatis.
- SERVER /api/invoices: (a) GET kini auto-sweep `sent`→`overdue` bila dueDate terlewati (aging & filter akurat); (b) action `create_standalone_invoice` — invoice manual tanpa project/quotation: wajib brand+company, nomor memakai invoicePrefix brand, MATA UANG = primaryCurrency brand (sinkron brand, bukan hardcode IDR), pajak parametrik (taxName+rate, null=tanpa pajak), due+notes, audit + push ke pimpinan; (c) action `update_invoice` — edit DRAFT saja (deskripsi/nominal/pajak/due/notes), pajak & total dihitung ulang server; (d) action `delete_payment` — koreksi pembayaran salah (level "full" di butuhkan), status dihitung ulang dalam transaksi, audit.
- KOMPONEN BARU invoice-print.tsx — InvoicePrintArea: dokumen invoice siap cetak (A4) dengan KOP SURAT brand sinkron penuh (letterheadHeader/Footer gambar, letterTemplate font+aksen, fallback identitas logo/tagline/alamat/kontak), badge status, tabel tagihan, subtotal/pajak/total, riwayat pembayaran + sisa tagihan (kwitansi internal), tanda tangan & footer brand.
- UI finance-module.tsx: tombol "Buat Invoice" (dialog: brand→hint prefix+mata uang, perusahaan fetch, pajak parametrik dari /api/taxes + "Tanpa Pajak", preview subtotal/pajak/total live), tombol "Ekspor CSV" (BOM UTF-8, pemisah ";" — semua kolom penting, daftar terfilter), tombol "Cetak" per baris & di detail sheet (render #print-area + window.print, pola sama dgn quotation), tombol "Edit" di detail sheet (draft saja), tombol hapus (Trash2) pada tiap riwayat pembayaran + AlertDialog konfirmasi; api-client bertambah createStandaloneInvoice/updateInvoice/deletePayment.
- RBAC DINAMIS: schema baru model ModulePermission (role+module unique, level: none|read|write|full, db push); lib server permissions.ts — DEFAULT_MATRIX (dari MODULE_META lama; direktur/super_admin full, hr→users read, dst), ensureDefaultPermissions (idempoten, aman P2002), loadPermissionMatrix (cache 30s), assertModuleLevel (gerbang bertingkat), invalidatePermissionCache.
- API /api/permissions: GET (butuh sesi) matriks penuh + daftar modul/level; PUT (super_admin/director + level users "full") simpan massal ter-validasi ketat (modul dikenal, level valid) via upsert transaksi + audit log; cache di-invalidasi agar langsung efektif.
- STORE: permissionMatrix (dari DB) + loadPermissions() dipanggil di page.tsx setelah sesi diketahui; canAccess kini DB-driven (fallback statis bila belum termuat); helper baru moduleLevel/canWrite/canFull + ACCESS_LEVEL_META (label+warna+deskripsi level utk UI). Navigasi sidebar, mobile nav, global search, deep-link push, guard modul — semuanya otomatis mengikuti matriks tanpa perubahan kode lain.

Kartu "Penjelasan Role" kini menghitung modul dari matriks DB. Daftar modul editor lengkap 13 (menambah reports & channels yang sebelumnya hilang).

Stage Summary:
- Modul Keuangan kini lengkap siklus penuh: BUAT (manual tanpa project/quotation, sinkron brand: prefix nomor + mata uang + kop surat) → EDIT (draft) → KIRIM → BAYAR (bertahap, anti lebih-bayar) → KOREKSI (hapus payment, status dihitung ulang) → CETAK (dokumen kop surat brand) → EKSPOR CSV; status overdue otomatis; aging akurat.
